## `AGO-6130-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-01`

- **Role:** High-RF-Exclusion-Zone Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** Shift Handover, Material Staging Verification & Bottleneck Takt-Time Balancing SOP for Geostationary Satellite Ground Station Dish Antenna Tracking
